# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Annual Union orders in the native Decisions menu.
COLDWAR_ORD_register = {
	COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { always = no }
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { always = no }
		is_good = yes
		selectable_mission = no
		fire_only_once = no
		days_mission_timeout = 180
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall_clock COLDWAR_ORD_sync_shortfall_all = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { always = no }
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { always = no }
		is_good = yes
		selectable_mission = no
		fire_only_once = no
		days_mission_timeout = 180
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents COLDWAR_ORD_sync_policies = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_ORD_procurement_agents = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		cost = 25
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			NOT = { tag = SOV }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_tolkachi_unlocked
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_mobilisation_ready
				COLDWAR_ORD_has_live_commission = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents }
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_procurement_agents days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						NOT = { tag = SOV }
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_tolkachi_unlocked
						COLDWAR_ORD_has_live_commission = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents }
					}
					set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents value = 1 days = 180 }
					activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_procurement_agents
					COLDWAR_ORD_sync_policies = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { always = no }
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { always = no }
		is_good = yes
		selectable_mission = no
		fire_only_once = no
		days_mission_timeout = 180
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime COLDWAR_ORD_sync_policies = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_ORD_overtime = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			NOT = { tag = SOV }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_storming_unlocked
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_mobilisation_ready
				COLDWAR_ORD_has_live_commission = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime }
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_stability = -0.05
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_overtime days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						NOT = { tag = SOV }
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_UNI_storming_unlocked
						COLDWAR_ORD_has_live_commission = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime }
					}
					add_stability = -0.05
					set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime value = 1 days = 180 }
					activate_mission = COLDWAR_ORD_policy_clock_overtime
					COLDWAR_ORD_sync_policies = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { always = no }
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { always = no }
		is_good = yes
		selectable_mission = no
		fire_only_once = no
		days_mission_timeout = 30
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 1 }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 0 }
				}
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_proposal_rifles
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}

	COLDWAR_ORD_objective_rifles = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		activation = { always = no }
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_fulfilled COLDWAR_ORD_ready_rifles = yes }
		}
		selectable_mission = no
		fire_only_once = no
		days_mission_timeout = 365
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = { COLDWAR_ORD_republic = no COLDWAR_ORD_human_country = no }
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { COLDWAR_ORD_neutralise_rifles = yes }
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_assistance_heading
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_debit_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_credit_tt }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_success_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_rifles days = 180 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_rifles days = 180 }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = { COLDWAR_ORD_succeed_rifles = yes }
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { COLDWAR_ORD_close_rifles = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_ORD_consign_rifles = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			NOT = { tag = SOV }

		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_stock_available
				check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type value = 0.00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						NOT = { tag = SOV }
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state = 2 }
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_kind = 2 }
						check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_weapons_type value = 0.00001 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
					COLDWAR_ORD_deliver_rifles = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}

	COLDWAR_ORD_inspect_rifles = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		target_root_trigger = { always = yes }
		targets = { UKR BLR EST LAT LIT ARM AZE GEO KAZ KYR TAJ TRK UZB MLV }
		target_non_existing = no
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { exists = yes COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			tag = SOV
			FROM = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state > 0 }
				check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = THIS.id }
			}
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			hidden_trigger = { always = no }
		}
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_rifles_state > 0 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_draft_target = THIS.id }
						}
						always = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}

				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading

		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes

				if = {

						NOT = { tag = SOV }

					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_ORD_missile_submarine_return_pending
				}
			}
